##### A week filled with AI vs Agency, Halloween Marketing, Gen-Z Goons vs. Cinema, Political Ad Spending, State of Luxury in China, and a lot of good marketing ideas + collaborations.

This newsletter is supported by our friends at [Tracksuit](https://substack.com/redirect/29725040-3277-4580-9bff-eb9b01574a23?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), the affordable, always-on brand tracking dashboard that helps marketers and agencies prove the impact of their brand work. 

> Brand & Advertising coverage from 12.10 — 24.10.2025, with new section covering ongoing sports season.

---

*In partnership with [Tracksuit](https://substack.com/redirect/29725040-3277-4580-9bff-eb9b01574a23?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)*

##### Make big bets, [defend](https://substack.com/redirect/29725040-3277-4580-9bff-eb9b01574a23?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) with data.

Put your hand up if you’ve ever had to fight tooth and nail to get a slightly unhinged campaign signed off 🙋

And then defend it because, yes, the polar bear breaks up with their human girlfriend because she’s using an aerosol deodorant, not the natural, eco-friendly stuff, but it’s growing brand awareness, okay?!? (this is a [legitimate campaign](https://substack.com/redirect/a446f236-24b9-42a4-ae4f-8e7dc204608b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), thanks [Wild](https://substack.com/redirect/642b79d1-e93d-44f1-8aae-68a126307de8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)).

Marketers are under the pump to go big or go home. They need to perform, entertain, grow *and* [prove](https://substack.com/redirect/29725040-3277-4580-9bff-eb9b01574a23?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) the ROI of brand spend.

If you’re trying to take a big bet instead of creating the same cookie cutter campaigns each quarter, you need evidence it improved awareness or shifted perception in your key audience segments — not just reached a million people.

[Tracksuit’s](https://substack.com/redirect/29725040-3277-4580-9bff-eb9b01574a23?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) always-on brand tracking that helps you prove the impact of your boldest campaign work over time…so you’ll get the budget for the next moderately chaotic idea.

* Google’s [Privacy Sandbox](https://substack.com/redirect/7f4a7512-bc6a-4d26-971e-e69eff9a0559?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is officially dead.
* WPP faces [US lawsuit](https://substack.com/redirect/0b07a781-5b2a-48dd-b5c2-c67472c3f058?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for allegedly [misleading](https://substack.com/redirect/5b3eed28-e950-4388-84ee-bd0651854fb1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) investors on financial performance.
	+ What’s [gone wrong](https://substack.com/redirect/817da867-5c68-4777-909e-de9f8fc4592a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) at WPP? The crown slips at world’s biggest advertising group.
* [Pitchfork](https://substack.com/redirect/0ff901ba-4126-40ad-8b44-ae89643904bd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is testing comments and reader scores on album reviews.
* Tracksuit’s *[Category Cardio Playbook](https://substack.com/redirect/be0081e8-b76a-48ba-afdd-fd92d24fd464?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* shares category and media consumption data across seven high-growth categories, from Running Shoes to Skincare.\*\*\*
* [L’Oréal](https://substack.com/redirect/e1495a9e-c199-4c2e-86d7-f3f05a4d068a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to acquire Kering Beauty portfolio for [$4.6 Billion](https://substack.com/redirect/e4ff2fe4-0ffd-4750-b0b0-e82dd6d44cd8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).

> 🤔 Report Break: Numerator reveals that 53% of parents of Gen Alpha children report giving them some sort of weekly allowance, [averaging at about $22](https://substack.com/redirect/50e7ceaa-e2dd-44f0-b1c1-f03eae34bd75?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g). The most popular items Gen Alpha consumers purchase with their allowance money are snacks (59%), entertainment (34%), electronics (31%) and beverages (31%).

* Akcelo [wins](https://substack.com/redirect/3afec602-a7ba-40a4-94fc-da4bcd957e01?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Queensland Airports pitch.

---

Back on Sunday. Until then…

<h3 dir="auto" style="direction: auto;text-align: start;unicode-bidi: isolate;font-family: 'SF Pro Display',-apple-system-headline,system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: normal;-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;-moz-osx-font-smoothing: antialiased;-webkit-appearance: optimizelegibility;-moz-appearance: optimizelegibility;appearance: optimizelegibility;margin: 4px 0 0;color: #777777;line-height: 24px;font-size: 18px;margin-top: 12px;">A week filled with AI vs Agency, Halloween Marketing, Gen-Z Goons vs. Cinema, Political Ad Spending, State of Luxury in China, and a lot of good marketing ideas + collaborations.</h3>

<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;margin-top: 0;"><span>This newsletter is supported by our friends at </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/29725040-3277-4580-9bff-eb9b01574a23?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Tracksuit</a><span>, the affordable, always-on brand tracking dashboard that helps marketers and agencies prove the impact of their brand work. </span></p>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Brand &amp; Advertising coverage from 12.10 — 24.10.2025, with new section covering ongoing sports season.</p></blockquote>

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>LVMH explores sale of its 50% stake in </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d49866bb-a4e1-43f5-824b-821d1969affb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Rihanna-backed Fenty Beauty</a><span>, sources say.</span></p></li>

Discovery</a><span> says it is considering a sale.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Target </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2aaa60f1-7bf5-4e0e-a17a-692f6f58d6cd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">eliminating</a><span> 1,800 positions, laying off 1,000 workers.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Mango founder’s son becomes </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/05d68f3e-2f17-4465-ad36-b34cbb412c08?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">alleged suspect</a><span> in fashion tycoon’s death.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/693418c5-d2ed-49f8-abb2-85441e433d69?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">The Goon Squad</a><span> — Loneliness, porn’s next frontier, and the dream of endless masturbation.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Netflix partners with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/9fd928a9-2cc5-4ff8-9447-f3721f1ce124?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Mattel and Hasbro</a><span> to bring KPop Demon Hunters to toy aisles</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>JPMorgan seeks </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/24fa7e49-a33c-43ff-ae14-cb8957fc1adc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">to attract</a><span> staff with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1a3c0cad-fc25-4e11-90fa-93611069d6a1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">$3 billion skyscraper</a><span> in New York.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: #e6e6e6;border: none;"/></div>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><em><span>In partnership with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/29725040-3277-4580-9bff-eb9b01574a23?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Tracksuit</a></em></p>
<h3 style="position: relative;font-family: 'SF Pro Display',-apple-system-headline,system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: bold;-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;-moz-osx-font-smoothing: antialiased;-webkit-appearance: optimizelegibility;-moz-appearance: optimizelegibility;appearance: optimizelegibility;margin: 1em 0 0.625em 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 1.16em;font-size: 1.375em;">
<span>Make big bets, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/29725040-3277-4580-9bff-eb9b01574a23?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: rgb(54,55,55);text-decoration: underline;">defend</a><span> with data.</span>
</h3>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Put your hand up if you’ve ever had to fight tooth and nail to get a slightly unhinged campaign signed off 🙋</p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>And then defend it because, yes, the polar bear breaks up with their human girlfriend because she’s using an aerosol deodorant, not the natural, eco-friendly stuff, but it’s growing brand awareness, okay?!? (this is a </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a446f236-24b9-42a4-ae4f-8e7dc204608b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">legitimate campaign</a><span>, thanks </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/642b79d1-e93d-44f1-8aae-68a126307de8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Wild</a><span>).</span></p>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
<td align="left" style="text-align: center;" width="568"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/37ea5ded-9705-4be7-b4a3-553b54cb0907?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="position: relative;flex-direction: column;align-items: center;padding: 0;width: auto;height: auto;border: none;text-decoration: none;display: block;margin: 0;" target="\_blank"><img alt="" data-attrs='{"src":"https://substack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/bbfa18dd-5fbd-4f48-8d76-dae73d239f57\_1600x896.png","srcNoWatermark":null,"fullscreen":null,"imageSize":null,"height":815,"width":1456,"resizeWidth":568,"bytes":null,"alt":"","title":"","type":null,"href":null,"belowTheFold":true,"topImage":false,"internalRedirect":null,"isProcessing":false,"align":null,"offset":false}' height="307.864010989011" src="https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/%24s\_!D3Jx!,w\_1100,c\_limit,f\_auto,q\_auto:good,fl\_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fbbfa18dd-5fbd-4f48-8d76-dae73d239f57\_1600x896.png" style="border: none !important;vertical-align: middle;display: block;-ms-interpolation-mode: bicubic;height: auto;margin-bottom: 0;width: auto !important;max-width: 100% !important;margin: 0 auto;" title="" width="550"/></a></td>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
</tr></tbody></table></figure></div>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Marketers are under the pump to go big or go home. They need to perform, entertain, grow </span><em>and</em><span> </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/29725040-3277-4580-9bff-eb9b01574a23?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">prove</a><span> the ROI of brand spend.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">If you’re trying to take a big bet instead of creating the same cookie cutter campaigns each quarter, you need evidence it improved awareness or shifted perception in your key audience segments — not just reached a million people.</p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/29725040-3277-4580-9bff-eb9b01574a23?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Tracksuit’s </a><span>always-on brand tracking that helps you prove the impact of your boldest campaign work over time…so you’ll get the budget for the next moderately chaotic idea.</span></p>
<p data-attrs='{"url":"https://www.gotracksuit.com?utm\_campaign=Creator%20Partnerships&amp;utm\_source=email&amp;utm\_medium=sponsored\_placement&amp;utm\_term=homepage&amp;utm\_content=The%20Social%20Juice","text":"Check out Tracksuit today!","action":null,"class":"button-wrapper"}' data-component-name="ButtonCreateButton" style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;text-align: center;cursor: pointer;border-radius: 4px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e8eb4d32-3c59-46bd-a4e4-92c55e63f30e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="font-family: system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';display: inline-block;box-sizing: border-box;border: none;font-size: 14px;line-height: 20px;font-weight: 600;margin: 0;opacity: 1;outline: none;white-space: nowrap;color: #ffffff !important;text-decoration: none !important;text-align: center;cursor: pointer;border-radius: 4px;background-color: #e85c57;padding: 12px 20px;height: auto;"><span style="color: #ffffff;text-decoration: none;">Check out Tracksuit today!</span></a></p>

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Aimé Leon Dore and Porsche unveil their </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f5c5843d-071f-4f55-b9d9-a52b86c3ad27?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">FW25 collection</a><span>, pairing 1990s design with motorsport details and refined outerwear.</span></p></li>

</li>
